panel_name = panel.get(\"panel_name\") or \"\"\n        aliases = panel.get(\"alias_labels\") or []\n        refs = {panel_name, *aliases}\n\n        matches = []\n        for r in panel_ref in refs:\n                matches.append(r)\n\n        if not matches:\n            raise ValueError(f\"No current contract_terms match for panel_code={panel.get('panel_code')} field_name=\"tests_per_lab_per_run_28_day\")\n\n        total_payment = base_payment + network_adj\n\n        def compute_model(*, runs_per_year: float, tests_per_lab_per_run: float):\n            annual_revenue = total_payment * active_labs * runs_per_year\n            annual_reagent_cost = reagent_cost_per_1000 * active_labs * tests_per_lab_per_run * runs_per_year / 1000.0\n            # shipper_cost_usd is per shipment/run; one shipment per run.\n            annual_shipper_cost = shipper_cost_usd * runs_per_year\n            annual_margin = annual_revenue - annual_reagent_cost - annual_shipper_cost\n            return {\n                \"runs_per_year\": runs_per_year,\n                \"annual_revenue\": annual_revenue,\n                \"annual_reagent_cost\": as_float(p.get(\"tests_per_lab_per_run_14_day\"), field_name=\"tests_per_lab_per_run_14_day\")\n        tests_28 = as_float(p.get(\"tests_per_lab_per_run_28_day\"), field_name=\"tests_per_lab_per_run_28_day\")\n\n        total_payment = base_payment + network_adj\n\n        def compute_model(*, runs_per_year: float, tests_per_lab_per_run: float):\n            annual_revenue = total_payment * active_labs * runs_per_year\n            annual_reagent_cost = reagent_cost_per_1000 * active_labs * tests_per_lab_per_run * runs_per_year / 1000.0\n            # shipper_cost_usd is per shipment/run; one shipment per run.\n            annual_shipper_cost = shipper_cost_usd * runs_per_year\n            annual_margin = annual_revenue - annual_reagent_cost - annual_shipper_cost\n            return {\n                \"runs_per_year\": runs_per_year,\n                \"annual_revenue\": annual_revenue,\n                \"annual_reagent_cost\": annual_reagent_cost,\n                \"annual_shipper_cost\": annual_shipper_cost,\n                \"annual_margin\": annual_margin,\n            }\n\n        m14 = compute_model(runs_per_year=26.0, tests_per_lab_per_run=tests_14)\n        m28 = compute_model(runs_per_year=13.0, tests_per_lab_per_run=tests_28)\n\n        panel_outputs.append(\n            {\n                \"panel_code\": \"total_annual_margin_difference_28_minus_14_usd\": round2(total_diff),\n        \"absolute_total_margin_difference_usd\": round2(abs_diff),\n    }\n\n    # Step 12: Decision.\n    decision = \"adopt_28_day\" if totals[\"absolute_total_margin_difference_usd\"] < 6000.0 else \"keep_14_day\"\n    justification = (\n        f\"Decision rule: adopt_28_day if absolute_total_margin_difference_usd < 6000.00; \"\n        f\"computed absolute difference is {totals['absolute_total_margin_difference_usd']:.2f}.\"\n    )\n\n    # Step 13: Build output JSON,             \"decision\": decision,\n                \"justification\": justification,\n                \"threshold_absolute_total_margin_difference_usd\": 6000.0,\n            },\n        },\n    }\n\n    with (ROOT / \"diagpanel_policy_report.json\").open(\"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(out, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Step 14: Summary markdown (4–8 non-empty lines).\n    with (ROOT /
